Sefirit
2014-01-30 16:04 UTC
#7
phantaskippy:

One-shot is based on how much damage one-shot cards add to the deck.  Unload is Expat's damage dealing card.I put her with Fixer and Chrono, because while Chrono has constant pings of damage, they add a ton of damage to the deck, Expat sits on the other side of that, but the value of Unload is similar to the value of Lightspeed Barrage, which is a big part of Tachyon's 5 in one-shot damage.  Fixer has Overdrive and Charge, which really boost his deck damage.Her damage boosting is all ammo, but it is one of the best situational damage boosting systems the game has. As for the importance of deck mechanics relative to other ratings:  I fully agree, which is why this wasn't intended to give an overall rating for heroes.  Tachyon is the obvious victim, as her deck is overall weak, except for the speed at which it plays, and that makes her incredibly good.

I see your point about total damage, but Unload is no where near as good as Lightspeed in practice.  Why?  you might ask.  Because Tachyon has card cycle built into her deck and can put mulitple burst cards in her trash a turn and 1/2 of the cards in her deck quallify.  If I see Tachyon go through a game where she plays fewer than 2 LSBs and any of them do fewer than 12 points of damage it is almost shocking.

Expat on the other hand.  Well I've playerd her more than 20 times and am seeing results like Donner.  I HAVE seen her Unload with more than two guns out, like 2-3 times in 20 plays. 

So in one case it happens multiple times per game, in the other it happens once every 8 games or so.

I see that you acknowledge this in the last sentence of your post, so I guess it is about how the rating system is done.  If you are going to rate a card on how effective it is in the game, I give LSB a really high score and a Unload a really low score.

PH
phantaskippy
2014-01-30 17:50 UTC
#8
Sefirit:

 

phantaskippy wrote:
One-shot is based on how much damage one-shot cards add to the deck.  Unload is Expat's damage dealing card.I put her with Fixer and Chrono, because while Chrono has constant pings of damage, they add a ton of damage to the deck, Expat sits on the other side of that, but the value of Unload is similar to the value of Lightspeed Barrage, which is a big part of Tachyon's 5 in one-shot damage.  Fixer has Overdrive and Charge, which really boost his deck damage.Her damage boosting is all ammo, but it is one of the best situational damage boosting systems the game has. As for the importance of deck mechanics relative to other ratings:  I fully agree, which is why this wasn't intended to give an overall rating for heroes.  Tachyon is the obvious victim, as her deck is overall weak, except for the speed at which it plays, and that makes her incredibly good.  
I see your point about total damage, but Unload is no where near as good as Lightspeed in practice.  Why?  you might ask.  Because Tachyon has card cycle built into her deck and can put mulitple burst cards in her trash a turn and 1/2 of the cards in her deck quallify.  If I see Tachyon go through a game where she plays fewer than 2 LSBs and any of them do fewer than 12 points of damage it is almost shocking.Expat on the other hand.  Well I've playerd her more than 20 times and am seeing results like Donner.  I HAVE seen her Unload with more than two guns out, like 2-3 times in 20 plays. So in one case it happens multiple times per game, in the other it happens once every 8 games or so.I see that you acknowledge this in the last sentence of your post, so I guess it is about how the rating system is done.  If you are going to rate a card on how effective it is in the game, I give LSB a really high score and a Unload a really low score.

The number of copies of the one-shot is considered.  I don't consider the deck mechanics, as there is a whole section devoted to that.  What I consider is if you have the card, what is the value of that card in different situations.  Unload can exceed LSB early game, late game LSB is better single target, while Unload is generally far more versatile in what it accomplishes.  One requires your trash to get stacked and not shuffled, the other requires gun cards in play.

I put them at even.  The fact that Tachyon's mechanics enable her to pull off LSB twice as often (average estimation) as Expat and Unload does not factor into the one-shot ranking.

BE
